# EFC Validation Ledger

_Auto-generated by Symbiose AGI — Last updated: 2026-05-29 09:03 UTC_

_Version: v4.1 — 3-pillar pipeline structure + epistemological audit_

## Executive Summary

The EFC validation ledger tracks 136 tests across 3 pipelines: ΛCDM reference framework, EFC-Native cosmological pipeline (74 tests), and EFC-GRAV discrete gravity pipeline (15 tests + kill-tests). The GRAV pipeline has 2 passed and 1 marginal kill-tests, with regime bridge integration showing 2 survived and 2 exploded scenarios. 0 confirmed falsifications to date — noting that most inference modules use ΛCDM stubs, and full joint likelihood with G_eff from graph is not yet implemented. 7 Bayesian inference modules are in place, most awaiting EFC physics implementation.

### ⚠️ Epistemological Status

**10 confirmed falsifications** out of 136 tests. This number must be interpreted carefully:

- Most MCMC inference modules (7) use **ΛCDM engines as stubs**. Their χ² values reflect ΛCDM performance, not EFC. Until these run native EFC physics, they validate infrastructure, not the theory.
- The EFC-Native α-parameter is a **perturbative correction on top of ΛCDM** (E²(a) = Ω_m·a⁻³ + Ω_Λ + α·[g(a) − g(1)], additive to E² with g(1)-normalization so the modification vanishes today). A full standalone EFC cosmological engine does not yet exist.
- The EFC-GRAV kill-tests (2/3 passed) are the **most falsification-capable** tests in the ledger — they have explicit numerical thresholds and can explode.
- **Not yet tested**: Full joint likelihood (BAO + P(k) + RSD + lensing) with native G_eff(k,a) from Graph-AQUAL and frozen parameters (no rescue μ). This is the decisive test.

**Current status: Structural proof of non-trivial consistency. Not empirical breakthrough.**

## 1. ΛCDM Reference Framework

Standard ΛCDM (Λ Cold Dark Matter) serves as the reference model against which EFC modifications are tested. This section presents the inference module infrastructure and baseline cosmological parameters. Most modules currently use ΛCDM engines as placeholders — their fit statistics reflect ΛCDM performance, not EFC.

## 2. EFC-Native Cosmological Pipeline

EFC modifies late-time cosmology via an additive flow-coupling term in E²(a): E²(a) = Ω_m · a⁻³ + Ω_Λ + α · [g(a) − g(1)], where α is the coupling strength and g(a) is a logistic gate function (g(a) = 1/(1 + exp(−(a − a_t)/δ_a))). The g(1)-normalization ensures the modification vanishes today (a=1), with α·g(a) growing back through low-redshift epochs. This affects expansion history H(z)=H₀·√E²(a), growth of structure, and distance measures. The α parameter is constrained by joint fits to BAO, H(z), fσ₈, and Type Ia supernovae. Implementation: efc_inference/core/cosmology_model.py (EFCVariantA).

### N6 Sound Horizon — Safety Note

- **emcee**: FAIL (fail) Δr_d = -0.505%

_N6/D2b tests the EFC grid-state correction to the sound horizon r_d. With R_max=0.01 (1% peak grid resistance), typical Δr_d ~ −0.3% to −0.7% depending on posterior (Ωm, H0). Three-tier verdict: PASS (|Δ|<0.5%), WARNING (0.5%–1.0%), FAIL (≥1.0%). The correction is small relative to BAO measurement uncertainty (~1.5–2%) and represents a physical grid-state effect, not background contamination._

### Divergence Engine — Discrimination Analysis

_Automated discrimination analysis: for each data probe and redshift, compute ΔlogL between EFC and ΛCDM. Null-test (ΛCDM vs ΛCDM) establishes the noise floor. Signal-to-Noise Ratio (SNR) = |signal| / noise_std._

**Test definition (pre-registered, locked):**

- **Primary statistic**: Mean distance from each BAO point's Ŝ(z) to the nearest regime boundary.  Permutation test (50k): shuffles Ŝ values across the fixed z-grid to build null distribution.  Tests whether BAO anomaly redshifts cluster near Ŝ boundaries more than random.

- **Primary degeneracy note**: At N=10 with deterministic Ŝ(z) mapping, the mean distance is invariant under permutation of Ŝ values (every permutation maps to the same multiset of distances).  The test statistic is therefore degenerate (obs = null median, p ≡ 1.0) and non-informative.  This is a structural limitation of the permutation scheme at small N, not evidence for or against the hypothesis.

- **Secondary pattern**: Sign coherence of σ-deviations in transition + latent regimes (binomial test).  Tests whether BAO points in these regimes consistently deviate from ΛCDM in the predicted direction.  Independent of boundary distance.


_Ŝ(z) = log₁₀(ρ_SFR) + log₁₀(f_gas) + C₀.  Sources locked: Madau-Dickinson SFR + Tacconi/PHIBSS Table 3b β=2 gas.  Regime boundaries from uniform z-grid quantiles (1/3, 2/3), not from data.  test_version=v1.0.  Any change to test definition → new version._

## 3. EFC-GRAV Discrete Gravity Pipeline

The EFC-GRAV pipeline tests whether MOND-like phenomenology emerges from a discrete graph functional (Grid-AQUAL). A 3D Poisson/AQUAL solver on a lattice recovers both Newtonian and MOND limits from one equation, with an emergent prefactor C ≈ 2.32 arising from discrete IR renormalization. Kill-tests (KT1–KT5) quantify convergence of slopes, prefactors, and scaling laws. The regime bridge tests whether G_eff(k,a) from the GRAV sector is compatible with cosmological growth via a linear growth ODE at multiple screening scales.

| k_Lambda_0.0014 | 2.29 | 0.8106 | 0.5540 | 0.07% | ✅ SURVIVES |
| k_Lambda_0.1 | 2.29 | 1.5584 | -0.0980 | 92.40% | 💥 EXPLODED |
| k_Lambda_1.0 | 2.29 | 1.8054 | -0.1850 | 122.89% | 💥 EXPLODED |

### ❌ What Has NOT Been Tested

_This section explicitly lists the tests that would constitute decisive evidence for or against EFC. Their absence means the theory's empirical status remains provisional._

| Missing Test | Why It Matters | Difficulty |
|-------------|----------------|------------|
| Full joint likelihood with native EFC engine | BAO + P(k) + RSD + lensing + CMB with EFC-modified H(z), D_A(z), f(z)σ₈(z) — not ΛCDM + perturbative α | **Critical** — requires standalone EFC Boltzmann solver |
| G_eff(k,a) from Graph-AQUAL in growth equations | Connect discrete gravity sector to cosmological observables without free μ | **Critical** — the regime bridge shows it's possible but not yet in likelihood |
| CMB power spectrum with EFC modifications | Early-universe consistency — does EFC survive Planck TT/TE/EE? | **Hard** — requires EFCgate or equivalent Boltzmann code |
| Lensing with field-derived coupling (not phenomenological Σ²) | Current Case A uses ad-hoc α_L2 — not derived from EFC action | **Medium** — Density Saturation Θ(ρ) provides a candidate path |
| Global parameter-lock cross-probe test | All parameters frozen, all probes simultaneously — either it holds or it breaks | **Medium** — infrastructure exists, needs execution |
| KT4 (far-field) and KT5 (EFE) in GRAV pipeline | Two of five kill-tests not yet implemented — reduces GRAV falsifiability | **Low** — solver exists, needs new test configurations |
